The al-Mizan principle informed the SHV-Bench two-sided success criterion but was not deployed as a diagnostic framework that generated independent findings. Its contribution
i﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿s the design principle, not a diagnostic procedure.
K﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿nown limits. The framework is a design principle, not a diagnostic. It tells you what a just evaluation
l﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍﻿ooks like; it does not tell you whether a specific evaluation achieves it.
I﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿nteraction with other frameworks. Directly related to the SHV-Bench protocol's two-sided success
c﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿riterion (Section 2.1) and to the Imputation Directionality Analysis (#41), which checks whether the
i﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿mputation rule treats both sides justly.
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿3. At-Tawbah (Correction and Return)
S﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍﻿ource: Qur'an 9 (at-Tawbah, "The Repentance"); the XZ Protocol's correction trajectory (DOI
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍﻿0.5281/zenodo.21500700) and the SHVVP's correction-as-evidence principle (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21583202).
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍﻿heoretical basis. Tawbah is not mere apology; it is a structural return -- acknowledging the error, ceasing
t﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿he action, and resolving not to repeat it. Applied to verification: a correction that does not acknowledge
t﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿he error's root cause, cease the defective pattern, and demonstrate non-recurrence is not tawbah -- it is
c﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿osmetic.
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿he audit question it answers. Does this correction address the root cause, or only the surface symptom?
H﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿as the defect pattern recurred after the correction?
O﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍﻿perational procedure.
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍﻿. For each correction, identify: the surface defect (what was fixed), the root cause (why the defect
e﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿xisted), and the recurrence guard (what is intended to avoid it happening again).
2﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿. Check: does the correction register name the root cause? If not, the correction is surface-only.
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿. Check: has the same defect class recurred in a subsequent round? If so, the tawbah is incomplete.
4﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍﻿. Check: is the recurrence guard testable (e.g., a new gate, a new scan)?
C﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍﻿omputed quantities. Root-cause-named fraction, recurrence count post-correction, guard-testability
f﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿raction.
P﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿roductivity verdict. PARTIAL. The Tawbah framework described the correction trajectory pattern
(﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿corrections that fix the symptom but not the cause recur) but was not associated with findings beyond what
t﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿he GAN Convergence framework's defect-migration analysis already captured.
K﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿nown limits. Root-cause identification is subjective. Two analysts may disagree on whether a correction
a﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿ddresses the root cause.
I﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿nteraction with other frameworks. Overlaps with GAN Convergence (#1) on the defect-migration
t﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌‌​‌​‍﻿rajectory. The Tawbah framework adds the "root cause" diagnostic that GAN does not explicitly model.
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍﻿4. Sidrat al-Muntaha (The Lote Tree of the Utmost Boundary)
S﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍﻿ource: Qur'an 53:14 ("At the Lote Tree of the Utmost Boundary"); the SHIELD paper's Sidrat Boundary
(﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍﻿SHIELD, DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22032727, Section 6); the self-referential incompleteness analogy described
i﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿n the synthesis paper (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21500700).
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍﻿heoretical basis. Sidrat al-Muntaha is the boundary beyond which even the angel Jibril could not pass
d﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​‌​‍﻿uring the Prophet's Night Journey (Isra and Mi'raj). Beyond it, knowledge belongs to God alone. Applied
t﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿o verification: every verification architecture has a boundary beyond which it cannot verify. Stating this
b﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​‌‍﻿oundary honestly is the Sidrat practice.
T﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿he audit question it answers. Does the artifact state what it cannot verify? Is the boundary honest
a﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿nd complete?

﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍﻿Operational procedure.
1﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍﻿. Identify the artifact's verification claims.
2﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌​​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​​‌‍﻿. For each claim, identify its verification boundary -- what is outside the claim's reach?
3﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​‌​‌‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​‌‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌​​‍﻿. Check: does the artifact state the boundary? If not, the artifact implicitly claims unbounded verification.

I﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​​‌​​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿ndependence: INDEPENDENT (addresses a distinct analytical question not reducible to another framework), SPECIALIZATION (applies an existing framework's analytical structure to a new domain), or OBSERVATION (an empirical pattern that may inform future framework development but does not yet constitute a reusable analytical procedure).
D﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​​‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌​​‌‍﻿erivation: PROSPECTIVE-UNVERIFIED (the framework's analytical concepts pre-existed its application, and the framework could in principle have yielded zero findings; however, timestamped pre-application
e﻿​​​​​​​​​‌​​​​​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​​‌​‌‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌​​‌‌‌​‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​‌​‌‍​​​​​​​​​‌‌‌​​‌​‍﻿vidence is not individually documented for all entries -- see the Derivation Ledger below) or RETROSPECTIVE (the entry was codified after the audit trajectory it describes; entries with YES verdicts had previously
